Boats arriving from Tan pay full inward dues if discharging at Shasi town, local trade dues if discharging near the West station; but in teSpn table they are all entered as 1ocal traders. In the same way boats from Shasi town to Tangyang pay full outwad dues, but are classed among local clearances. Boats arriving from Tangyang for other ports pay full outward dues. They are entered in the Shipping table first as local entries, then as interport clearances. Boats arriving from other ports for Tangyang have usually no dues to pay, but in the Shipping table they are entered, ilrstly, as interport entries and, secondly, as local clearances. II.-IMPORTS. PART III. NOTIFICATIONS, During the Quarter ended September 80, 1911
288 OU8TOMSGAZETTE. [JULY-8RPT., NOTICES TO MARINERS. CHINA SEA. No. 514. SHANGHAI DISTRICT. TSUNGMING CROSSING. ALTaRATIONS IN BtOYAGE. NoTon is hereby given of the following changes in the buoyage of the Tsungming Orossing:- The Second Crossing Gas-lighted Buoy -has been discontinued. A gas-lighted buoy, painted red, and to be known as the West Spit Buoy, has been moored in 16 feet of water at low water of spring tides, with Washaway Beacon bearing 8. 73j&#39; E., distant 2.6 miles. This buoy shows an occulting white light every 8 seconds. An unlighted buoy, surmounted by a triangular shape and painted black, to be known as the Second Crossing Buoy, has been placed in 14 feet of water at low water of spring tides, with Washaway Beacon bearing S. so&#39; E., distant 2.1 miles. These buoys should be pased at a distance of I cable, aocording to their colour. CAUTION. Veasels cannot steer a straight course between the East Entrance Gas-lig4ted Buoy and the West Spit Ga&amp;amp;-lighted Buoy. Snixoz, I5h Jul 19 ft
1911.TO]oS. 289 No. 515. TIENTSIN DISTRICT. ESTABLISHMENT OF TAKU BAR GAS LIGHT-VESSEL AND OUTER GAS BUOY. NOTICE is hereby given that the old lightship Tacu has been replaced by a gas-lighted light-vessel, and that a gas-lighted outer.buoy has been established. The new gas light-vessel is of steel, 81) feet in length and 25 feet beam, painted red, with the word TAKU on her sides in white letters. The light is exhibited from a lantern carried by an iron column, is dioptric, revolving, of the fourth order, and shows a white flash every to seconds. The power of the flash is 45,000 candles. The centre of the light is 35 feet above the water, and the light should be visible in clear weather at a distance of ii nautical miles. In foggy or thick weather a gong will be sounded at i-minute intervals. If the lightship be driven from her proper pbsition, the usual light will not be shown, but a fixed red light will be exhibited at each end of the vessel during the night and a red flag hoisted by day. A gas-lighted buoy surmounted by a conical superstructure, painted black, and showing an occulting white light every 8 seconds, thus:- Light .. ...... . ...4 seconds, to be known as the Outer Gs-gted Buoy, has been placed in 13 feet of water at low water of spring tides, 1.55 miles N. W~ E. of the Entrance Buoy, (unlighted). SHIANGR1Ai, 6th September&#39; i19i1. 19
290 CUOveox GAZETTE. [.TtLY--Pr.,111} No. 516. SHANGHAI DISTRICT. TSUNGMING OROSSING. Noncon is hereby given of the following changes in the buoyage of the Tsungming Crossing:-- An East Entrance Gas-lighted Buoy, painted red, has been placed in a position from which Washaway Beacon bears 8. i* E., and is distant 4.6 miles, to mark the starboard side of the channel for inward-bound vessels. A First Crossing Buoy (ulighted), painted black and surmounted by a eylindrical shape, has been placed in a position from which Washaway Beacon bears S. 27-l E., and is distant 4.5 miles, to mark the port side of the channel for inward-bound vessels. A Second Crossing Gas-lighted Buoy, painted red, has been placed in a position from which Washaway Beacon bears S. 36* E., and is distant 5.05 miles, to mark the starboard side of the channel for inward-bound vessels. A West Spit Gas-lighted Buoy, painted in red and black vertical stripes, has been placed in a position from which Washaway Beacon bears 8. 652* E., and is distant 7.8 miles, to mark the port side of the channel for inward-bound vessels.. NOTE. The gas buoys marking the new crossing will be lighted on the 23rd instant, the date on which the buoys marking the old crossing will be discontinued. SHANHA, igth September .191. &amp;lt;A A In
